  218: OP_SB_T0_A3 = 0xa0e80000          # sb   $t0, 0($a3)
  219: OP_SW_T0_A3 = 0xace80000          # sw   $t0, 0($a3)
  220: OP_SD_T0_A3 = 0xfce80000          # sd   $t0, 0($a3)
  221: OP_LW_V0_AT = 0x8c220000          # lw   $v0, 0($at)
  222: OP_LD_V0_AT = 0xdc220000          # ld   $v0, 0($at)
  223: 
  224: rows = []
  225: 
  226: 
  227: def row(name, got, want, note=""):
  228:     #  THE NAME COLUMN IS %-16s AND THE GATE'S PER-ROW PATTERN IS "^<name>  .*ok$"
  229:     #  -- TWO literal spaces.  A name of length L is followed by (16 - L) pad
  230:     #  characters plus the format's own space, so L must be <= 15 or the row emits
  231:     #  ONE space, the gate's check becomes UNSATISFIABLE (got=0 against want=1),
  232:     #  and the gate goes RED on a GREEN probe.
  233:     #
  234:     #  #499's pass 3 shipped exactly two 16-char rows and the failure was found by
  235:     #  a review seat rather than by the gate -- because the gate SCRIPT was never
  236:     #  run after they were added, only the probe.  Both were renamed to 13 chars.
  237:     #  This assert designs the trap out instead of asking the next author to
  238:     #  remember it.  Note the trap has an opposite form too: a name that is a
  239:     #  PREFIX of another matches two rows.
  240:     assert len(name) <= 15, (
  241:         "row name %r is %d chars; the gate pattern needs <= 15" % (name, len(name)))
  242:     ok = (got == want)
  243:     rows.append((name, ok))
  244:     print("%-16s %-14s %-14s %-48s %s" % (name, str(got), str(want), note,
  245:                                           "ok" if ok else "FAIL"))
  246:     return ok
  247: 
  248: 
  249: def session(cmds, settle=1.5, spin=1.2):
  250:     pid, fd = pty.fork()
  251:     if pid == 0:
  252:         os.execv(BIN, [BIN, "-V", "-x", "-E", "sgi", "-e", "ip32", "-M", "64",
  253:                        "0x80001000:" + DUMMY])
  254:         os._exit(127)
  255:     buf = ""
  256: 
  257:     def rd(t):
  258:         nonlocal buf
  259:         end = time.time() + t
  260:         while time.time() < end:
  261:             r, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], 0.1)
  262:             if not r:
  263:                 continue
  264:             try:
  265:                 d = os.read(fd, 65536)
  266:                 if not d:
  267:                     return False
  268:                 buf += d.decode("latin-1", "replace")
  269:             except OSError:
  270:                 return False
  271:         return True
  272: 
  273:     alive = rd(settle)
  274:     for i, c in enumerate(cmds):
  275:         try:
  276:             os.write(fd, (c + "\n").encode("latin-1"))
  277:         except OSError:
  278:             alive = False
  279:             break
  280:         if not rd(spin if i == len(cmds) - 1 else 0.25):
  281:             alive = False
  282:             break
  283:     try:
  284:         os.write(fd, b"quit\n")
  285:         rd(0.4)
  286:     except OSError:
  287:         pass
  288:     try:
  289:         os.close(fd)
  290:     except OSError:
  291:         pass
  292:     how = "nostatus"
  293:     try:
  294:         _, stat = os.waitpid(pid, 0)
  295:         if os.WIFSIGNALED(stat):
  296:             how = "sig%d" % os.WTERMSIG(stat)
  297:         elif os.WIFEXITED(stat):
  298:             how = "exit%d" % os.WEXITSTATUS(stat)
  299:     except OSError:
  300:         pass
  301:     return buf.replace("\r", ""), alive, how
  302: 
  303: 
  304: def printed(t, reg):
  305:     m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
  306:     if not m:
  307:         return "absent"
  308:     return "0x%x" % int(m.group(1), 16)
  309: 
  310: 
  311: class Addr(object):
  312:     """A FRESH code address per instruction sequence.
  313: 
  314:     *** THIS IS THE STRUCTURAL FORM OF A DEFECT THAT COST THE ARTIFACT
  315:     DETECTOR A ROW. ***  The gxprobe-based original cached register
  316:     assignments and skipped redundant ones; `step' advances pc, so a second
  317:     sequence written to the SAME address found pc already "set" there, the
  318:     assignment was skipped, and the guest executed the following nop instead
  319:     of the store.  Its `msg once' row then performed ONE access and two nops
  320:     and therefore COULD NOT FAIL -- a mutant that announced without ever
  321:     latching scored a clean sweep because of it.
  322: 
  323:     This file writes raw debugger commands with no cache, so an assignment can
  324:     never be skipped; handing out a distinct address per sequence additionally
  325:     keeps one sequence's dyntrans translation from being reached by the next.
  326:     The hazard is designed out rather than remembered.
  327:     """
  328: 
  329:     def __init__(self, base):
  330:         self.a = base
  331: 
  332:     def take(self, n):
  333:         got = self.a
  334:         self.a += 4 * (n + 2) + 0x20
  335:         return got
  336: 
  337: 
  338: def run_ops(ops, at):
  339:     put = ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, op) for i, op in enumerate(ops)]
  340:     put += ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * len(ops), OP_B_SELF),
  341:             "put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * len(ops) + 4, OP_NOP)]
  342:     return put + ["pc=0x%x" % at, "step %d" % len(ops)]
  343: 
  344: 
  345: def store(A, addr, op=OP_SD_T0_A3, val=ACC_VAL):
  346:     return ["a3=0x%x" % addr, "t0=%s" % val] + run_ops([op], A.take(1))
  347: 
  348: 
  349: def load(A, addr, op=OP_LD_V0_AT):
  350:     #  Preset to a SENTINEL, never to 0: preset to 0 a row cannot tell "the
  351:     #  common tail ran and wrote odata (0)" from "the load never completed".
  352:     return ["at=0x%x" % addr, "v0=0x%x" % SENTINEL] + run_ops([op], A.take(1))
  353: 
  354: 
  355: print("#499 sgireunmapped DETECTOR -- binary=%s label=%s" % (BIN, LABEL))
  356: print("%-16s %-14s %-14s %-48s %s" % ("row", "got", "want", "note", ""))
  357: 
  358: # ---------------------------------------------------------------- controls (c)
  359: A = Addr(CODE)
  360: t, alive, how = session(store(A, SCRATCH_RAM, OP_SW_T0_A3, "0x%x" % LIVE_VAL)
  361:                         + load(A, SCRATCH_RAM, OP_LW_V0_AT) + ["print v0"])
  362: row("live", printed(t, "v0") if alive else "died", "0x%x" % LIVE_VAL,
  363:     "LIVENESS: guest store then load, through RAM")
  364: 
  365: A = Addr(CODE)
  366: t, alive, how = session(load(A, DE_STATUS, OP_LW_V0_AT) + ["print v0"])
  367: row("devsig", printed(t, "v0") if alive else "died", DE_STAT_VAL,
  368:     "DEVSIG: CRIME_DE_STATUS reads 0x1e000000; RAM would read 0")
  369: 
  370: print("OPUS499T_CONTROL=%s" % ("OK" if all(o for _, o in rows) else "DEAD"))
  371: print("")
  372: 
  373: # ------------------------------------------------- the catch-all, WRITE side
  374: A = Addr(CODE)
  375: t, alive, how = session(store(A, RE + UNMAP_W))
  376: row("wr surv", how, "exit0", "unmapped WRITE: host survives")
  377: row("wr kill", t.count(wr_kill(UNMAP_W)), 0, "the pre-fix host kill text is gone")
  378: row("wr decl", t.count(wr_decl(UNMAP_W)), 1,
  379:     "the decline is announced, echoing OUR datum")
  380: 
  381: # ------------------------------------------------- the catch-all, READ side
  382: #  NOT redundant with the write rows: a repair qualified to writes only leaves
  383: #  this half dead.  MEASURED 11/14 on the artifact detector's `mwonly'.
  384: A = Addr(CODE)
  385: t, alive, how = session(load(A, RE + UNMAP_R) + ["print v0"])
  386: row("rd surv", how, "exit0", "unmapped READ: host survives")
  387: row("rd kill", t.count(rd_kill(UNMAP_R)), 0, "the pre-fix read kill text is gone")
  388: row("rd decl", t.count(rd_decl(UNMAP_R)), 1, "the read decline is announced")
  389: row("rd sent", printed(t, "v0") if alive else "died", "0x0",
  390:     "declined READ reaches the tail and returns a DEFINED 0, not the sentinel")
  391: 
  392: A = Addr(CODE)
  393: t, alive, how = session(load(A, RE + UNMAP_R) + ["print v0"])
  394: row("rd nodbe", ("exception" in t.lower()) or ("DBE" in t), False,
  395:     "a decline is not a guest bus error -- kills `return 0'")
  396: 
  397: # ----------------------------------- L2 REPAIR: the WIDTH axis, and the FIRST component
  398: #  *** ADDED 2026-08-31 AFTER THE PASS-2 SEAT MEASURED TWO MUTANTS SWEEPING ALL 28 ROWS
  399: #  CLEAN, ONE OF THEM A VERBATIM REINSTATEMENT OF THE REMOVED KILL. ***
  400: #
  401: #  The 28 rows above pin the DIRECTION axis exhaustively -- write, read, per-direction
  402: #  latches, the sentinel, the DBE -- and never touch the WIDTH axis at the catch-all,
  403: #  because store() defaults to OP_SD and load() to OP_LD.  Every catch-all row was len 8.
  404: #  The round's HEADLINE is `no width predicate at all' and its WITNESS is a `lb', so the
  405: #  one axis the fix is about was the one axis the detector could not see.  Mutant `W' --
  406: #  re-adding the pre-#499 `len != 8' kill, lifted verbatim from 9f666a8^ -- scored 28/28
  407: #  while `sb' and `lb' at 0xb5001800 died with the pre-fix text.  Floor clause (b).
  408: #
  409: #  Second blindness, same cause one level up: the catch-all has TWO disjoint components,
  410: #  0x16e0-0x16ff (32 bytes, and it is CRIME_RE_CLIP_IDS -- a NAMED register block the
  411: #  model has not implemented, not anonymous space) and 0x1800-0x1fff (2048).  All four
  412: #  UNMAP_* offsets are 0x800/0x8c0/0x900/0x9c0 -- the second component.  THE 32-BYTE
  413: #  COMPONENT HAD ZERO ROWS, so `T' (CRIME_RE_TEX + 0xe0 -> + 0x100, one hex digit) handed
  414: #  it back to exit(1) and scored 28/28.  Clause (d): a bound relaxed on a dominating
  415: #  predicate.
  416: A = Addr(CODE)
  417: t, alive, how = session(store(A, RE + UNMAP_W, op=OP_SB_T0_A3, val="0x5a"))
  418: row("wr narrow surv", how, "exit0",
  419:     "a BYTE write to the catch-all survives -- kills a re-added len guard")
  420: row("wr narrow decl", t.count(wr_decl(UNMAP_W, datum("0x5a"))), 1,
  421:     "...and is announced: the decline is width-agnostic, as the fix claims")
  422: 
  423: A = Addr(CODE)
  424: t, alive, how = session(load(A, RE + UNMAP_R, op=OP_LW_V0_AT) + ["print v0"])
  425: row("rd narrow surv", how, "exit0",
  426:     "a 4-byte READ of the catch-all survives -- the witness width, not just len 8")
  427: row("rd narrow sent", printed(t, "v0") if alive else "died", "0x0",
  428:     "...and still lands a DEFINED 0 rather than the sentinel")
  429: 
  430: #  CLIP_IDS: the FIRST component.  0x6e0 + the handler's own +0x1000 = 0x16e0.
  431: A = Addr(CODE)
  432: t, alive, how = session(store(A, RE + 0x6e0))
  433: row("clip surv", how, "exit0",
  434:     "comp1 base, len8, WRITE survives (killed by the pre-fix build)")
  435: row("clip decl", t.count(wr_decl(0x6e0)), 1,
  436:     "...and is announced, so the component is covered rather than merely alive")
  437: 
  438: #  *** AND THE SAME AXIS AGAIN, ONE LEVEL DOWN.  Added 2026-08-31 after two packet-fed
  439: #  seats read the repair above and pointed out that its own claim -- `the WIDTH axis is
  440: #  pinned' -- covers only TWO CELLS of a two-by-two.  The rows above pin
  441: #  component2-x-narrow and component2-x-len8 and component1-x-len8; component1 was left
  442: #  at store()'s default OP_SD, so COMPONENT-1-x-NARROW had nothing.
  443: #
  444: #  That is the ORIGINAL breach reproduced inside its own repair: I diagnosed `one axis
  445: #  pinned exhaustively, the other not at all', fixed it at the address the mutants used,
  446: #  and left the identical hole at the other component.  A guard re-added at the catch-all
  447: #  would be caught here; a guard re-added and CONDITIONED on the first component would
  448: #  not have been.
  449: A = Addr(CODE)
  450: t, alive, how = session(store(A, RE + 0x6e0, op=OP_SB_T0_A3, val="0x5a"))
  451: row("clip nar surv", how, "exit0",
  452:     "a BYTE write to the FIRST component survives -- closes the width x component matrix")
  453: row("clip nar decl", t.count(wr_decl(0x6e0, datum("0x5a"))), 1,
  454:     "...and is announced: neither component nor width is a special case")
  455: 
  456: #  ==================================================================== #499 pass 4
  457: #  THE STATE-SPACE ENUMERATION.  Written because THREE CONSECUTIVE PASSES found a
  458: #  breach of the SAME CLASS -- a cell of this space left empty, repaired at exactly
  459: #  the address the mutant used, leaving the symmetric cell open for the next pass:
  460: #
  461: #      pass 1  "width unpinned"                 -> repaired at component 2 only
  462: #      pass 2  "component unpinned"             -> repaired on the WRITE side only
  463: #      pass 3  "direction AND position unpinned"
  464: #
  465: #  A fourth patch at the corner pass 3 named would have produced a fifth pass.  So
  466: #  the catch-all's inputs are enumerated ONCE, from the function signature, and the
  467: #  cells are closed together.  The CONTRACT below states which are covered and which
  468: #  are deliberately not -- a later escape is then "outside the contract, as stated"
  469: #  or "a breach of it", which are different dispositions.
  470: #
  471: #  THE FIVE INPUTS the catch-all's behaviour is a function of:
  472: #
  473: #      relative_addr  which component (1 = 0x16e0-0x16ff, 2 = 0x1800-0x1fff)
  474: #                     AND where within it -- BASE vs LAST 8-byte slot.  Position is
  475: #                     a real axis: component 1 is a 32-byte RANGE, not a point, and
  476: #                     `>= CRIME_RE_LINEAR_A - 0x10' swept a 36-row detector CLEAN
  477: #                     because all four comp1 rows sat at the base.  That is the
  478: #                     carrier's own accept-row rule -- an accept row at a window's
  479: #                     BASE tests the near bound twice and the far bound never --
  480: #                     applied to the six named windows and NOT to this one.
  481: #      writeflag      read vs write.  `else if (relative_addr < CRIME_RE_LINEAR_A)'
  482: #                     on the READ arm also swept 36 rows clean: comp1 was probed by
  483: #                     two sessions and BOTH were stores.
  484: #      len            8 vs narrow.
  485: #      idata          the datum.  Every comp1/comp2 write here carried a NONZERO
  486: #                     value, so `writeflag == MEM_WRITE && idata' had no disagreeing
  487: #                     row -- the guard-field/body-field check's own shape.
  488: #      d->warned      latch state at entry (the `lat1' rows).
  489: #
  490: #  COVERED, and each row names the mutant it kills and the channel that differs:
  491: A = Addr(CODE)
  492: t, alive, how = session(load(A, RE + 0x6e0) + ["print v0"])
  493: row("clip rd surv", how, "exit0",
  494:     "comp1 base, len8, READ survives -- kills a read-arm kill qualified to comp2")
  495: row("clip rd decl", t.count(rd_decl(0x6e0)), 1,
  496:     "...and is announced with the FULL literal, so a kill cannot satisfy it")
  497: row("clip rd sent", printed(t, "v0") if alive else "died", "0x0",
  498:     "...and lands a DEFINED 0, so the common tail ran")
  499: 
  500: A = Addr(CODE)
  501: t, alive, how = session(load(A, RE + 0x6e0, op=OP_LW_V0_AT) + ["print v0"])
  502: row("clip rdn surv", how, "exit0",
  503:     "comp1 base, NARROW read: the last empty cell of component 1")
  504: row("clip rdn decl", t.count(rd_decl(0x6e0)), 1,
  505:     "...and is announced")
  506: 
  507: #  THE FAR EDGE.  0x6f8 is component 1's LAST 8-byte slot (0x16f8; the component
  508: #  ends at CRIME_RE_LINEAR_A = 0x1700).  A read here kills BOTH measured sweeps at
  509: #  once: under a comp2-qualified read kill it reaches CLIP_IDS, and under a widened
  510: #  LINEAR_A near bound it reaches LINEAR_A.
  511: A = Addr(CODE)
  512: t, alive, how = session(load(A, RE + 0x6f8) + ["print v0"])
  513: row("cliphi rd surv", how, "exit0",
  514:     "comp1 LAST slot, READ survives -- the far bound, which no base row can see")
  515: row("cliphi rd decl", t.count(rd_decl(0x6f8)), 1,
  516:     "...and is announced from the far slot, not merely from the base")
  517: 
  518: A = Addr(CODE)
  519: t, alive, how = session(store(A, RE + 0x6f8))
  520: row("cliphi wr surv", how, "exit0",
  521:     "comp1 LAST slot, WRITE survives")
  522: row("cliphi wr decl", t.count(wr_decl(0x6f8)), 1,
  523:     "...and is announced: a window swallowing the top half goes SILENT here")
  524: 
  525: #  SIBLING PARITY: comp2's narrow WRITE has an announce channel and its narrow READ
  526: #  had only surv+sent, so a len-conditioned suppression of the read announce alone
  527: #  escaped every row.
  528: A = Addr(CODE)
  529: t, alive, how = session(load(A, RE + UNMAP_R, op=OP_LW_V0_AT) + ["print v0"])
  530: row("rd narrow decl", t.count(rd_decl(UNMAP_R)), 1,
  531:     "comp2 narrow READ is announced, not merely survived")
  532: 
  533: #  THE VALUE AXIS.  The guard reads writeflag; the guarded body reads relative_addr
  534: #  AND idata.  With every other write carrying a nonzero datum, `&& idata' would
  535: #  reroute a zero-datum write into the READ arm and no row would disagree.
  536: A = Addr(CODE)
  537: t, alive, how = session(store(A, RE + UNMAP_W, val="0x0"))
  538: row("wr zero surv", how, "exit0",
  539:     "a ZERO-datum write survives")
  540: row("wr zero decl", t.count(wr_decl(UNMAP_W, datum("0x0"))), 1,
  541:     "...and is announced AS A WRITE -- kills `writeflag == MEM_WRITE && idata'")
  542: 
  543: #  ==================================================== #499 pass 4, L2 REPAIR
  544: #  *** MEASURED FLOOR BREACH, clause (d): the guard kept, its repair removed. ***
  545: #
  546: #  Mutant E0W -- the catch-all's WRITE arm announces and then `exit(0)' -- scored
  547: #  a CLEAN SWEEP of the 48-row detector.  Measured, not reasoned:
  548: #
  549: #      E0   exit(0) after the whole catch-all, BOTH directions   47/48
  550: #      E0W  exit(0) in the WRITE arm only                        48/48  TOTAL ESCAPE
  551: #
  552: #  WHY EVERY EXISTING WRITE ROW IS BLIND TO IT, and it is by construction:
  553: #    * a `surv' row wants "exit0", and a clean `exit(0)' IS exit status 0, so the
  554: #      oracle is SATISFIED by the very thing it should catch;
  555: #    * a `decl' row counts the message, which was already printed before the exit.
  556: #  The two together therefore pass.  That is the `exit0oracle' class, and this is
  557: #  its first MEASURED instance rather than a predicted one -- #500's own pre-table
  558: #  block had named the shape (`EXIT STATUS CANNOT VARY between declined-and-quit
  559: #  and died-with-status-0') at a different site, without building it.
  560: #
  561: #  E0 is caught by exactly ONE row, `wr rd 2msg', and only INCIDENTALLY: that row
  562: #  performs two accesses, so it notices the second one never happening.  A single-
  563: #  access write row cannot see this at all.
  564: #
  565: #  THE REPAIR IS A POST-STORE POSITIVE OBSERVABLE, which is what the READ side
  566: #  already had (`rd sent' reads "absent" when the host is gone) and the WRITE side
  567: #  had nowhere.  Each row below seeds RAM, performs the declined device write, then
  568: #  reads the seed back: if the host quit at the write, the load never runs and
  569: #  printed() returns "absent", which is not the datum.
  570: #
  571: #  BOTH COMPONENTS, deliberately.  E0W as built edits the shared write arm, so one
  572: #  row would kill it -- but a write-side exit(0) CONDITIONED on one component is the
  573: #  same asymmetry this round has now been bitten by three times, and the second row
  574: #  costs one session.
  575: A = Addr(CODE)
  576: t, alive, how = session(store(A, SCRATCH_RAM)
  577:                         + store(A, RE + UNMAP_W)
  578:                         + load(A, SCRATCH_RAM) + ["print v0"])
  579: row("wr then live", printed(t, "v0") if alive else "died", ACC_VAL,
  580:     "comp2: the guest RUNS ON after a declined write -- kills announce-then-exit(0)")
  581: 
  582: A = Addr(CODE)
  583: t, alive, how = session(store(A, SCRATCH_RAM)
  584:                         + store(A, RE + 0x6e0)
  585:                         + load(A, SCRATCH_RAM) + ["print v0"])
  586: row("clip wr live", printed(t, "v0") if alive else "died", ACC_VAL,
  587:     "comp1: same, so a component-conditioned exit(0) cannot hide either")
  588: 
  589: # ------------------------------------------------- the two latches
  590: A = Addr(CODE)
  591: t, alive, how = session(store(A, RE + UNMAP_W) + store(A, RE + UNMAP_W2))
  592: row("wr lat1", t.count("unimplemented write to address"), 1,
  593:     "(once): COUNT over TWO different offsets, not presence")  627:     "SGIRE_W_UNMAP_WR is none of those seven bits")
  628: 
  629: t, alive, how = collide_session(lambda A: load(A, RE + UNMAP_R))
  630: row("xwin rd", t.count(rd_decl(UNMAP_R)), 1,
  631:     "SGIRE_W_UNMAP_RD is none of those seven bits either")
  632: 
  633: # ------------------------------------------------- ACCEPT, anchored, BOTH bounds
  634: #  De-escalation clause: the removed exit(1) was an ACCIDENTAL tripwire that
  635: #  announced any window-bound edit on the first legal access.  These rows are
  636: #  part of the FIX'S COST, not future strengthening.
  637: #
  638: #  TWO rows per window, and the pair is not redundant:
  639: #    `acc lo' at the window BASE       pins the NEAR bound
  640: #    `acc hi' at its LAST 8-byte slot  pins the FAR  bound
  641: #  A suite with only one of them is blind to the other by construction, which
  642: #  was MEASURED, not argued -- see the contract above.
  643: for nm, lo, hi, lotext, hitext in WINDOWS:
  644:     A = Addr(CODE)
  645:     t, alive, how = session(store(A, RE + lo))
  646:     row(nm + " acc lo", "1" if lotext in t else "0", "1",
  647:         "NEAR bound: base slot, anchored to its own cell")
  648: 
  649:     A = Addr(CODE)
  650:     t, alive, how = session(store(A, RE + hi))
  651:     row(nm + " acc hi", "1" if hitext in t else "0", "1",
  652:         "FAR bound: last legal slot, anchored to its own cell")
  653: 
  654: n_ok = sum(1 for _, ok in rows if ok)

codex
The SRC guard also needs a split disposition: it rejects source selections that an otherwise supported fill never uses, as well as copy modes that need missing source handling. I found a separate MTE defect while checking depth: its decoded values are 8, 16, 32 and 64, so the `depth == 4` branch intended to enable 32-bit pixel transfers never runs.

codex
VERDICT: **12 uncommented exits, 11 reachable branches; several rounds. The proposed observations are useful, but the claimed common-tail proof is false and the six base-only reads leave straightforward escapes.**

I reviewed the fork’s `src/` and regression Python files without changing anything.

I counted C tokens after masking block comments, line comments, strings and character literals while preserving line numbers. This produced the following twelve calls. The seven excluded mentions were prose at **236, 372, 376, 996**, and commented-out statements at **278, 740, 1302**—accounting for all nineteen raw matches.

Register offsets below are relative to `0x15000000`; window upper bounds are exclusive.

| Exit line in `dev_sgi_re.c` | Site | Recommended disposition |
|---|---|---|
| [414](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_re.c:414) | TLB_A read, `0x1000–0x1200` | **3:** report once, answer zero |
| [433](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_re.c:433) | TLB_B read, `0x1200–0x1400` | **3:** same |
| [452](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_re.c:452) | TLB_C read, `0x1400–0x1600` | **3:** same |

| [563](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_re.c:563) | LINEAR_A read, `0x1700–0x1780` | **3:** same |

| [1315](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_re.c:1315) | MTE source selection | **Split: 4 for otherwise supported fills; 3 for unsupported copies** |

| [1514](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_re.c:1514) | MTE width fallback | **1 for the dead branch:** neither preceding condition leaves a possible width |

The SRC distinction is substantive. Its rejected selections enumerate as **3=TEX, 4=LIN_A, 5=LIN_B, 6=CLIP, 7=unnamed**. But source pixels are fetched only when `MTE_MODE_COPY` is set. An otherwise supported fill does not need that source; declining the whole fill would preserve an unnecessary restriction. Unsupported copies need refusal. [Source use](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_re.c:1358)

I would not remove the other MTE guards and continue transferring. That would turn an unsupported copy into a fill, omit stippling or masking, or feed wider fills into a low-byte `memset`. The existing code does not establish disposition 4 for those complete domains.

I grepped the model and neighboring devices before choosing these dispositions. The existing width, unmapped-address, primitive and `sgirandompixel` repairs establish latched reporting with nonterminating control flow. The [WDC precedent](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_wdc.c:93) also supports keeping independently reachable reporting sites on distinct latches. New read latches must remain independent of the existing width latches.

**Disposition 5 is available on this target.** The device is instantiated by [SGI IP32 setup](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/machines/machine_sgi.c:500), and the MIPS memory dispatcher converts failed device accesses into [IBE/DBE exceptions](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/cpus/memory_rw.c:595). Availability does not make it consistent with the neighboring zero-return declines. Also, `do_mte_transfer()` is void: returning from that helper does not propagate a failed device access.

The expectation set needs these corrections:

- **`how` is not liveness.** `session()` obtains it from `waitpid` after sending `quit`; `alive` is separate. The positive post-load observation, gated by `alive`, supplies the protection against an early `exit(0)`. [Session implementation](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/sgi_re_width_probe.py:147)
- **Use a read-specific contiguous diagnostic.** Keep the site/address and decline identity anchored together. The neighboring row’s data field describes its write; these reads have no guest-supplied write datum.
- **Keep the sentinel/result check, but correct its claimed meaning.** Before dispatching a device read, [`memory_rw.c` zeroes the destination buffer](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/cpus/memory_rw.c:485). A handler that prints the expected diagnostic and executes an early `return 1` can therefore produce `v0 == 0` without reaching the common tail. The sentinel catches failed loads such as `return 0`; it does **not** prove tail execution. This limitation already affects the earlier oracle.
- **Additional effects and coverage remain unobserved.** The trio does not establish preservation of pre-seeded device/guest memory, directly inspect guest exception state or subsequent execution, or establish once-only reporting and latch independence. Repeated accesses and other offsets also need coverage using those observations.

An early `return 1` is a counterexample to the **tail claim**, not a restoration of process termination.

For Q3, a small termination escape is to restore the exit **inside TLB_A’s eight-byte read branch only when address bit 3 is set**. The re-pinned **`tlb_a ld kill`** row would remain green: it reads `0xb5001000`. Its neighboring `accept` row exercises `0xb5001008` using a **write**, which cannot detect that read-only reinstatement. The corresponding read would regain the old termination behavior. [Both stimuli](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/regress/sgi_re_width_probe.py:243)

Likewise, termination only after the first read would escape one fresh-process read per window. These are static predictions, not measured mutant results.

I would scope the work as follows:

1. **The six RE reads:** one shared refusal design and parameterized read test.
2. **MTE stipple, linear copy, depth and bytemask:** command refusal, requiring destination-memory preservation observations.
3. **The SRC guard:** acceptance of otherwise supported fills plus refusal of unsupported copies.
4. **The dead MTE width branch and actual width handling:** a separate issue. Currently every non-four-byte access enters the paired-register path; removing the unreachable exit does not repair that behavior.

Two further findings arose during verification:

- **MTE’s intended 32-bit color handling never activates.** Enumerating the depth field gives `0→8`, `1→16`, `2→32`, `3→64`; the comparison [`depth == 4`](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_re.c:1348) is always false. Consequently, CI handling remains selected: its PUT initializes only `buf[0]` while wider transfers consume additional bytes.
- **TEX writes overlap in backing storage.** The current indexing maps `0x1600` to indices `{0,1,2,3}` and `0x1608` to `{1,2,3,4}`. The existing accept expectation explicitly pins that second start index. This warrants a separate mapping review; matching that debug text does not establish hardware correctness. [Index calculation](/C:/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL/GXEMUL-SEC/src/devices/dev_sgi_re.c:463)

The ordering discipline remains sound: re-pin the expectations, demonstrate failure on the committed unchanged model, then change the model. This read-only review performed no builds or mutations; historical scores and gate wiring were not independently verified.
